1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a elements algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 482. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 205/5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to elements theory, we decompose the problem into 482 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 205. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
